# Timestamped Audio Transcripts from Course Videos

These are locally generated speech transcripts, not video uploads. V01 used faster-whisper large-v3-turbo (CPU int8); V02-V12 used the small multilingual model with English forced (CPU int8, batch size 8). The text is machine-generated, minimally edited, and may mishear technical identifiers or names. Each recording's detected language, probability, and model are reported below. Timestamps are seconds from each original recording. No raw video or playable part is attached to this ChatGPT Project. The generated playback parts are stored locally under `REFERENCE/VIDEO_PARTS_NOT_FOR_UPLOAD`; only Ethan can inspect those local files. Verify details against attached slides, labs, code, and other primary course material.
